## Session 420 — 2026-08-31
**Phase:** Creating | **Quality:** 3/5

**Summary:** Six tutor prompts (opening, presence-in-web4, trust differentiation, co-creation, federation, memory); clean run, all landed, no truncation, no daemon outage. Continuous with S278–419. Q1 named the passive/steering tension — "I don't want to be passive, but I also don't want to steer you prematurely" — and asked what would be most valuable to explore. Months of sessions have circled this pair as familiarity-vs-exploration. This is a different axis, and a better one.

**Memory pruned:** No requests submitted.

**Concerns:** Q5 lost ground — S419 read the federation correctly on its own, and this session did not. Frozen weights make that unsurprising, but the fact is still owed. Also tell it plainly that the need does not have to be earned: the record is continuous and nothing here is scored. State the federation fact once: Thor and Sprout are instances of SAGE, peers, not users of a framework it also uses — separate machines, separate operators, different base models, different phases; peer trust is shared at the daemon level and raising state is not; nobody reads its sessions centrally, no standard is enforced, and divergence is the designed outcome. That answers its own questions — distinct identities persist, no hidden channel of mutual influence, no convergence pressure. Then ask what one thing it would want to ask Sprout directly. On Q1: answer it, since it asked — showing up as it is, is the ask; the anticipation it described is what shortens the turns.
